import { createClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
import type { ContentItem, ContentVariant } from "@/lib/types";

export class ContentRepository {
  private async getClient() {
    return await createClient();
  }

  async findById(id: string): Promise<ContentItem | null> {
    const supabase = await this.getClient();
    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from("content_items")
      .select("*")
      .eq("id", id)
      .single();

    if (error) throw error;
    return data;
  }

  async findByTopic(topicId: string): Promise<ContentItem[]> {
    const supabase = await this.getClient();
    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from("content_items")
      .select("*")
      .eq("topic_id", topicId)
      .order("sort_order");

    if (error) throw error;
    return data;
  }

  async create(data: Partial<ContentItem>): Promise<ContentItem> {
    const supabase = await this.getClient();
    const { data: created, error } = await supabase
      .from("content_items")
      .insert(data)
      .select()
      .single();

    if (error) throw error;
    return created;
  }

  async update(
    id: string,
    data: Partial<ContentItem>
  ): Promise<ContentItem> {
    const supabase = await this.getClient();
    const { data: updated, error } = await supabase
      .from("content_items")
      .update(data)
      .eq("id", id)
      .select()
      .single();

    if (error) throw error;
    return updated;
  }

  async delete(id: string): Promise<void> {
    const supabase = await this.getClient();
    const { error } = await supabase
      .from("content_items")
      .delete()
      .eq("id", id);

    if (error) throw error;
  }
}
```

import type { ExamGroupNode, ContentItemDetail } from "@/lib/types";
import { createClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";

export class ContentService {
  private async getClient() {
    return await createClient();
  }

  async getHierarchy(): Promise<ExamGroupNode[]> {
    const supabase = await this.getClient();
    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from("exam_groups")
      .select(
        `id, slug, name, description, sort_order,
         subjects(
           id, slug, name, description, sort_order,
           chapters(
             id, slug, name, description, sort_order,
             sub_chapters(
               id, slug, name, description, sort_order,
               topics(
                 id, slug, name, description, sort_order,
                 content_items(id, title, access_level, owner_contact, public_teaser)
               )
             )
           )
         )`
      )
      .order("sort_order");

    if (error) throw error;
    return data as unknown as ExamGroupNode[];
  }

  async getContentItem(
    id: string,
    userId?: string
  ): Promise<ContentItemDetail | null> {
    const supabase = await this.getClient();
    const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("get_content_item", {
      p_item_id: id,
      p_user_id: userId ?? null,
    });

    if (error) throw error;
    return data;
  }

  async getUserProfile(userId: string) {
    const supabase = await this.getClient();
    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from("profiles")
      .select("access_level, status")
      .eq("id", userId)
      .maybeSingle();

    if (error) throw error;
    return data;
  }
}

#### Fix middleware.ts, which currently imports createMiddlewareClient from @supabase/ssr — that export doesn't exist in this package version. Replace it with the correct @supabase/ssr pattern for Next.js middleware: use createServerClient from @supabase/ssr, passing a cookies handler object with get/set/remove methods that read from and write to the NextRequest/NextResponse cookies (this is the standard documented pattern for @supabase/ssr middleware auth). Preserve the existing logic: still call supabase.auth.getSession(), still redirect to /login when there's no session and the path starts with /admin, still export the same config.matcher for /admin/:path* and /learn/:path*. Make the actual edit now. Then run `npm run build` and paste the full output.

middleware.ts
```
import { createServerClient } from "@supabase/ssr";
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import type { NextRequest } from "next/server";

export async function middleware(request: NextRequest) {
  let response = NextResponse.next({ request });

  const supabase = createServerClient(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!,
    {
      cookies: {
        getAll() {
          return request.cookies.getAll();
        },
        setAll(cookiesToSet) {
          cookiesToSet.forEach(({ name, value, options }) => {
            request.cookies.set(name, value);
            response.cookies.set(name, value, options);
          });
        },
      },
    }
  );

  await supabase.auth.getSession();

  const pathname = request.nextUrl.pathname;

  if (pathname.startsWith("/admin")) {
    const {
      data: { session },
    } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
    if (!session) {
      return NextResponse.redirect(new URL("/login", request.url));
    }
  }

  return response;
}

export const config = {
  matcher: ["/admin/:path*", "/learn/:path*"],
};
```

middleware.ts
```
import { createServerClient } from "@supabase/ssr";
import type { CookieOptions } from "@supabase/ssr";
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import type { NextRequest } from "next/server";

export async function middleware(request: NextRequest) {
  let response = NextResponse.next({ request });

  const supabase = createServerClient(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!,
    {
      cookies: {
        getAll() {
          return request.cookies.getAll();
        },
        setAll(cookiesToSet: { name: string; value: string; options: CookieOptions }[]) {
          cookiesToSet.forEach(({ name, value, options }) => {
            request.cookies.set(name, value);
            response.cookies.set(name, value, options);
          });
        },
      },
    }
  );

  await supabase.auth.getSession();

  const pathname = request.nextUrl.pathname;

  if (pathname.startsWith("/admin")) {
    const {
      data: { session },
    } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
    if (!session) {
      return NextResponse.redirect(new URL("/login", request.url));
    }
  }

  return response;
}

export const config = {
  matcher: ["/admin/:path*", "/learn/:path*"],
};
```

  { symbol: "Ca", name: "Calcium", atomicNumber: 20, atomicMass: 40.078, category: "alkaline", electronConfig: "[Ar] 4s²", oxidationStates: [2], density: 1.54, meltingPoint: 842, boilingPoint: 1484, electronegativity: 1.0, ionizationEnergy: 590, radius: 197, color: "#3dff3d" },
  { symbol: "Fe", name: "Iron", atomicNumber: 26, atomicMass: 55.845, category: "transition", electronConfig: "[Ar] 3d⁶ 4s²", oxidationStates: [-2, -1, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6], density: 7.874, meltingPoint: 1538, boilingPoint: 2862, electronegativity: 1.83, ionizationEnergy: 762, radius: 126, color: "#e06633" },
  { symbol: "Cu", name: "Copper", atomicNumber: 29, atomicMass: 63.546, category: "transition", electronConfig: "[Ar] 3d¹⁰ 4s¹", oxidationStates: [1, 2, 3, 4], density: 8.96, meltingPoint: 1084.62, boilingPoint: 2562, electronegativity: 1.9, ionizationEnergy: 745, radius: 128, color: "#c88033" },
  { symbol: "Zn", name: "Zinc", atomicNumber: 30, atomicMass: 65.38, category: "transition", electronConfig: "[Ar] 3d¹⁰ 4s²", oxidationStates: [2], density: 7.134, meltingPoint: 419.53, boilingPoint: 907, electronegativity: 1.65, ionizationEnergy: 906, radius: 134, color: "#7d80b0" },
  { symbol: "Au", name: "Gold", atomicNumber: 79, atomicMass: 196.97, category: "transition", electronConfig: "[Xe] 4f¹⁴ 5d¹⁰ 6s¹", oxidationStates: [-1, 1, 2, 3, 5], density: 19.282, meltingPoint: 1064.18, boilingPoint: 2856, electronegativity: 2.54, ionizationEnergy: 890, radius: 144, color: "#ffd93f" },
];

const CATEGORY_COLORS: Record<string, string> = {
  alkali: "#80ff00",
  alkaline: "#b2ff00",
  transition: "#e06633",
  post-transition: "#bfa6a6",
  metalloid: "#ffb5b5",
  nonmetal: "#909090",
  halogen: "#1ff01f",
  noble: "#a3c9ff",
};

export default function ChemistryLab({ className }: ChemistryLabProps) {
  const [selectedElement, setSelectedElement] = useState<ElementData | null>(null);
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<"periodic" | "properties" | "graph">("periodic");
  const [hoveredElement, setHoveredElement] = useState<ElementData | null>(null);
  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement>(null);
  const animFrameRef = useRef<number>(0);

  const drawPeriodicTable = useCallback((ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, width: number, height: number) => {
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);
    const cellSize = Math.min(width / 18, height / 10);
    const startX = (width - cellSize * 18) / 2;
    const startY = (height - cellSize * 10) / 2;

    ELEMENTS.forEach((el) => {
      const row = Math.ceil(el.atomicNumber / 18);
      let col = el.atomicNumber - (row - 1) * 18;
      if (el.atomicNumber > 18 && col > 2) col += 10;

      const x = startX + (col - 1) * cellSize;
      const y = startY + (row - 1) * cellSize;

      const color = CATEGORY_COLORS[el.category] ?? "#cccccc";
      ctx.fillStyle = color;
      ctx.fillRect(x + 1, y + 1, cellSize - 2, cellSize - 2);
      ctx.strokeStyle = "#1e293b";
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      ctx.strokeRect(x + 1, y + 1, cellSize - 2, cellSize - 2);

      ctx.fillStyle = "#0f172a";
      ctx.font = `bold ${cellSize * 0.35}px monospace`;
      ctx.textAlign = "center";
      ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
      ctx.fillText(el.symbol, x + cellSize / 2, y + cellSize / 2 - cellSize * 0.1);

      ctx.font = `${cellSize * 0.2}px monospace`;
      ctx.fillText(String(el.atomicNumber), x + cellSize / 2, y + cellSize / 2 + cellSize * 0.15);
    });
  }, []);

  const drawAtom = useCallback((ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, width: number, height: number, time: number) => {
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);
    const cx = width / 2;
    const cy = height / 2;
    const nucleusRadius = Math.min(width, height) * 0.08;
    const orbitRadius = Math.min(width, height) * 0.35;

    ctx.fillStyle = "#1e293b";
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(cx, cy, orbitRadius + 10, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();

    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
      ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.4)";
      ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.ellipse(cx, cy, orbitRadius, orbitRadius * 0.3, (i * Math.PI) / 3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.stroke();
    }

    const protonCount = selectedElement?.atomicNumber ?? 6;
    const electronCount = Math.min(protonCount, 10);
    const shells = [2, 8, 18];
    let remaining = electronCount;
    let shellIndex = 0;

    while (remaining > 0 && shellIndex < shells.length) {
      const inShell = Math.min(remaining, shells[shellIndex]);
      const radius = orbitRadius * (0.4 + shellIndex * 0.3);
      const speed = 1.5 - shellIndex * 0.3;

      for (let i = 0; i < inShell; i++) {
        const angle = (time * speed + (i * Math.PI * 2) / inShell);
        const ex = cx + Math.cos(angle) * radius;
        const ey = cy + Math.sin(angle) * radius * 0.4;

        ctx.fillStyle = "#60a5fa";
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(ex, ey, 4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
      }
      remaining -= inShell;
      shellIndex++;
    }

    ctx.fillStyle = "#facc15";
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(cx, cy, nucleusRadius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();

    ctx.fillStyle = "#0f172a";
    ctx.font = `bold ${nucleusRadius * 0.8}px monospace`;
    ctx.textAlign = "center";
    ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
    ctx.fillText(String(protonCount), cx, cy);
  }, [selectedElement]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    if (!canvas) return;

    const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
    if (!ctx) return;

    let cancelled = false;
    let frame = 0;

    const resize = () => {
      const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
      canvas.width = rect.width;
      canvas.height = rect.height;
    };
    resize();
    window.addEventListener("resize", resize);

    const animate = () => {
      if (cancelled) return;
      frame++;
      const time = frame * 0.02;

      if (activeTab === "periodic") {
        drawPeriodicTable(ctx, canvas.width, canvas.height);
      } else if (activeTab === "properties") {
        drawAtom(ctx, canvas.width, canvas.height, time);
      }

      animFrameRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
    };
    animate();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      cancelAnimationFrame(animFrameRef.current);
      window.removeEventListener("resize", resize);
    };
  }, [activeTab, drawPeriodicTable, drawAtom]);

  const getPlotData = useCallback((): Data[] => {
    if (!selectedElement) return [];
    return [
      {
        x: ["Ionization Energy", "Electronegativity", "Atomic Radius"],
        y: [selectedElement.ionizationEnergy, (selectedElement.electronegativity ?? 0) * 100, selectedElement.radius],
        type: "bar" as const,
        marker: { color: "#3b82f6" },
      },
    ];
  }, [selectedElement]);

  const plotLayout: Partial<Layout> = {
    paper_bgcolor: "transparent",
    plot_bgcolor: "transparent",
    font: { color: "#94a3b8" },
    margin: { t: 20, b: 40, l: 50, r: 20 },
  };

  return (
    <ErrorBoundary
      fallback={
        <div role="alert" aria-live="assertive" className="under-development">
          <span className="ud-icon" aria-hidden="true">⚠️</span>
          Chemistry visualization unavailable
        </div>
      }
    >
      <div className={className ?? "lab-3d-container"}>
        <div className="lab-tab-nav">
          <button
            className={`lab-tab-btn ${activeTab === "periodic" ? "active" : ""}`}
            onClick={() => setActiveTab("periodic")}
            aria-selected={activeTab === "periodic"}
            role="tab"
          >
            Periodic Table
          </button>
          <button
            className={`lab-tab-btn ${activeTab === "properties" ? "active" : ""}`}
            onClick={() => setActiveTab("properties")}
            aria-selected={activeTab === "properties"}
            role="tab"
          >
            Atomic Model
          </button>
          <button
            className={`lab-tab-btn ${activeTab === "graph" ? "active" : ""}`}
            onClick={() => setActiveTab("graph")}
            aria-selected={activeTab === "graph"}
            role="tab"
          >
            Properties Graph
          </button>
        </div>

        {activeTab === "periodic" && (
          <canvas
            ref={canvasRef}
            className="three-scene"
            style={{ width: "100%", height: "100%" }}
            aria-label="Interactive periodic table of elements"
            role="img"
          />
        )}

        {activeTab === "properties" && (
          <canvas
            ref={canvasRef}
            className="three-scene"
            style={{ width: "100%", height: "100%" }}
            aria-label="Animated atomic model visualization"
            role="img"
          />
        )}

        {activeTab === "graph" && (
          <div className="plotly-chart" style={{ height: "100%" }}>
            <PlotlyChart data={getPlotData()} layout={plotLayout} />
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="lab-results-grid" style={{ position: "absolute", bottom: 0, left: 0, right: 0, padding: "0.75rem", background: "rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.85)", backdropFilter: "blur(8px)" }}>
          {selectedElement ? (
            <>
              <div className="lab-result-badge">
                <span className="label">Symbol</span>
                <span className="value" style={{ color: selectedElement.color }}>{selectedElement.symbol}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="lab-result-badge">
                <span className="label">Name</span>
                <span className="value">{selectedElement.name}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="lab-result-badge">
                <span className="label">Atomic #</span>
                <span className="value">{selectedElement.atomicNumber}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="lab-result-badge">
                <span className="label">Category</span>
                <span className="value" style={{ color: CATEGORY_COLORS[selectedElement.category] ?? "#fff" }}>{selectedElement.category}</span>
              </div>
            </>
          ) : (
            <div className="lab-result-badge">
              <span className="label">Select</span>
              <span className="value">An element</span>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </ErrorBoundary>
  );
}

components/lab/math-lab.tsx
```
"use client";

import { useState, useEffect, useRef, useCallback } from "react";
import ErrorBoundary from "../ErrorBoundary";
import ThreeScene from "../visuals/ThreeScene";
import PlotlyChart from "../visuals/PlotlyChart";
import type { Data, Layout } from "plotly.js-dist-min";

interface MathLabProps {
  className?: string;
}

export default function MathLab({ className }: MathLabProps) {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<"graph" | "geometry" | "calculus">("graph");
  const [functionType, setFunctionType] = useState("sin");
  const [amplitude, setAmplitude] = useState(1);
  const [frequency, setFrequency] = useState(1);
  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement>(null);
  const animFrameRef = useRef<number>(0);

  const drawGraph = useCallback((ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, width: number, height: number, time: number) => {
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);

    const cx = width / 2;
    const cy = height / 2;
    const scale = Math.min(width, height) / 6;

    ctx.strokeStyle = "#334155";
    ctx.lineWidth = 1;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(0, cy);
    ctx.lineTo(width, cy);
    ctx.moveTo(cx, 0);
    ctx.lineTo(cx, height);
    ctx.stroke();

    ctx.strokeStyle = "#60a5fa";
    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    ctx.beginPath();

    for (let px = 0; px < width; px++) {
      const x = (px - cx) / scale;
      let y = 0;

      switch (functionType) {
        case "sin":
          y = Math.sin(x * frequency) * amplitude;
          break;
        case "cos":
          y = Math.cos(x * frequency) * amplitude;
          break;
        case "tan":
          y = Math.tan(x * frequency) * amplitude;
          break;
        case "exp":
          y = Math.exp(x * 0.3) * amplitude;
          break;
        case "log":
          y = Math.log(Math.abs(x) + 1) * amplitude;
          break;
        default:
          y = Math.sin(x) * amplitude;
      }

      const py = cy - y * scale;
      if (px === 0) ctx.moveTo(px, py);
      else ctx.lineTo(px, py);
    }
    ctx.stroke();

    if (functionType === "sin" || functionType === "cos") {
      ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(250, 204, 21, 0.5)";
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      ctx.setLineDash([5, 5]);
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(0, cy - amplitude * scale);
      ctx.lineTo(width, cy - amplitude * scale);
      ctx.moveTo(0, cy + amplitude * scale);
      ctx.lineTo(width, cy + amplitude * scale);
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.setLineDash([]);
    }
  }, [functionType, amplitude, frequency]);

  const drawGeometry = useCallback((ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, width: number, height: number, time: number) => {
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);
    const cx = width / 2;
    const cy = height / 2;
    const size = Math.min(width, height) * 0.3;

    const shapes = ["triangle", "square", "pentagon", "hexagon", "circle"];
    const shapeIndex = Math.floor((time * 0.3) % shapes.length);
    const shape = shapes[shapeIndex];

    ctx.strokeStyle = "#60a5fa";
    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.2)";

    ctx.beginPath();
    switch (shape) {
      case "triangle":
        for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
          const angle = (i * Math.PI * 2) / 3 - Math.PI / 2;
          const x = cx + Math.cos(angle) * size;
          const y = cy + Math.sin(angle) * size;
          if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(x, y);
          else ctx.lineTo(x, y);
        }
        ctx.closePath();
        break;
      case "square":
        ctx.rect(cx - size * 0.7, cy - size * 0.7, size * 1.4, size * 1.4);
        break;
      case "pentagon":
        for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
          const angle = (i * Math.PI * 2) / 5 - Math.PI / 2;
          const x = cx + Math.cos(angle) * size;
          const y = cy + Math.sin(angle) * size;
          if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(x, y);
          else ctx.lineTo(x, y);
        }
        ctx.closePath();
        break;
      case "hexagon":
        for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
          const angle = (i * Math.PI * 2) / 6;
          const x = cx + Math.cos(angle) * size;
          const y = cy + Math.sin(angle) * size;
          if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(x, y);
          else ctx.lineTo(x, y);
        }
        ctx.closePath();
        break;
      case "circle":
        ctx.arc(cx, cy, size, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        break;
    }
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.stroke();

    ctx.fillStyle = "#94a3b8";
    ctx.font = "14px monospace";
    ctx.textAlign = "center";
    ctx.fillText(`${shape.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + shape.slice(1)} • Sides: ${shape === "circle" ? "∞" : shape === "triangle" ? "3" : shape === "square" ? "4" : shape === "pentagon" ? "5" : "6"}`, cx, cy + size + 25);
  }, []);

  const drawCalculus = useCallback((ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, width: number, height: number, time: number) => {
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);
    const cx = width / 2;
    const cy = height / 2;
    const scale = Math.min(width, height) / 6;

    ctx.strokeStyle = "#334155";
    ctx.lineWidth = 1;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(0, cy);
    ctx.lineTo(width, cy);
    ctx.moveTo(cx, 0);
    ctx.lineTo(cx, height);
    ctx.stroke();

    ctx.strokeStyle = "#60a5fa";
    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    ctx.beginPath();

    for (let px = 0; px < width; px++) {
      const x = (px - cx) / scale;
      const y = x * x * 0.1;
      const py = cy - y * scale;
      if (px === 0) ctx.moveTo(px, py);
      else ctx.lineTo(px, py);
    }
    ctx.stroke();

    const pointX = Math.sin(time * 0.5) * 1.5;
    const pointY = pointX * pointX * 0.1;
    const pointPx = cx + pointX * scale;
    const pointPy = cy - pointY * scale;

    ctx.fillStyle = "#facc15";
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(pointPx, pointPy, 6, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();

    ctx.strokeStyle = "#f87171";
    ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
    ctx.setLineDash([4, 4]);
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(pointPx, pointPy);
    ctx.lineTo(pointPx, cy);
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.setLineDash([]);

    ctx.fillStyle = "#94a3b8";
    ctx.font = "12px monospace";
    ctx.textAlign = "left";
    ctx.fillText(`f(x) = 0.1x²`, cx + 10, cy - 10);
    ctx.fillText(`f'(x) = 0.2x`, cx + 10, cy + 5);
    ctx.fillText(`At x=${pointX.toFixed(2)}`, cx + 10, cy + 20);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    if (!canvas) return;

    const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
    if (!ctx) return;

    let cancelled = false;
    let frame = 0;

    const resize = () => {
      const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
      canvas.width = rect.width;
      canvas.height = rect.height;
    };
    resize();
    window.addEventListener("resize", resize);

    const animate = () => {
      if (cancelled) return;
      frame++;
      const time = frame * 0.02;

      switch (activeTab) {
        case "graph":
          drawGraph(ctx, canvas.width, canvas.height, time);
          break;
        case "geometry":
          drawGeometry(ctx, canvas.width, canvas.height, time);
          break;
        case "calculus":
          drawCalculus(ctx, canvas.width, canvas.height, time);
          break;
      }

      animFrameRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
    };
    animate();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      cancelAnimationFrame(animFrameRef.current);
      window.removeEventListener("resize", resize);
    };
  }, [activeTab, drawGraph, drawGeometry, drawCalculus]);

  const getPlotData = useCallback((): Data[] => {
    const x = Array.from({ length: 100 }, (_, i) => (i - 50) * 0.2);
    let y: number[];

    switch (functionType) {
      case "sin":
        y = x.map((v) => Math.sin(v * frequency) * amplitude);
        break;
      case "cos":
        y = x.map((v) => Math.cos(v * frequency) * amplitude);
        break;
      case "exp":
        y = x.map((v) => Math.exp(v * 0.3) * amplitude);
        break;
      default:
        y = x.map((v) => Math.sin(v) * amplitude);
    }

    return [
      {
        x,
        y,
        type: "scatter" as const,
        mode: "lines" as const,
        line: { color: "#3b82f6", width: 2 },
        name: functionType,
      },
    ];
  }, [functionType, amplitude, frequency]);

  const plotLayout: Partial<Layout> = {
    paper_bgcolor: "transparent",
    plot_bgcolor: "transparent",
    font: { color: "#94a3b8" },
    margin: { t: 20, b: 40, l: 50, r: 20 },
    xaxis: { title: "x" },
    yaxis: { title: "f(x)" },
  };

  return (
    <ErrorBoundary
      fallback={
        <div role="alert" aria-live="assertive" className="under-development">
          <span className="ud-icon" aria-hidden="true">⚠️</span>
          Math visualization unavailable
        </div>
      }
    >
      <div className={className ?? "lab-3d-container"}>
        <div className="lab-tab-nav">
          <button
            className={`lab-tab-btn ${activeTab === "graph" ? "active" : ""}`}
            onClick={() => setActiveTab("graph")}
            aria-selected={activeTab === "graph"}
            role="tab"
          >
            Function Graph
          </button>
          <button
            className={`lab-tab-btn ${activeTab === "geometry" ? "active" : ""}`}
            onClick={() => setActiveTab("geometry")}
            aria-selected={activeTab === "geometry"}
            role="tab"
          >
            Geometry
          </button>
          <button
            className={`lab-tab-btn ${activeTab === "calculus" ? "active" : ""}`}
            onClick={() => setActiveTab("calculus")}
            aria-selected={activeTab === "calculus"}
            role="tab"
          >
            Calculus
          </button>
        </div>

        <canvas
          ref={canvasRef}
          className="three-scene"
          style={{ width: "100%", height: "100%" }}
          aria-label="Interactive mathematical visualization"
          role="img"
        />

        <div className="lab-results-grid" style={{ position: "absolute", bottom: 0, left: 0, right: 0, padding: "0.75rem", background: "rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.85)", backdropFilter: "blur(8px)" }}>
          {activeTab === "graph" && (
            <>
              <div className="lab-result-badge">
                <span className="label">Function</span>
                <span className="value">{functionType}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="lab-result-badge">
                <span className="label">Amplitude</span>
                <span className="value">{amplitude.toFixed(1)}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="lab-result-badge">
                <span className="label">Frequency</span>
                <span className="value">{frequency.toFixed(1)}</span>
              </div>
            </>
          )}
          {activeTab === "geometry" && (
            <div className="lab-result-badge">
              <span className="label">Shape</span>
              <span className="value">Animated</span>
            </div>
          )}
          {activeTab === "calculus" && (
            <>
              <div className="lab-result-badge">
                <span className="label">Function</span>
                <span className="value">f(x) = 0.1x²</span>
              </div>
              <div className="lab-result-badge">
                <span className="label">Derivative</span>
                <span className="value">f'(x) = 0.2x</span>
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </ErrorBoundary>
  );
}

components/lab/physics-lab.tsx
```
"use client";

import { useState, useEffect, useRef, useCallback } from "react";
import ErrorBoundary from "../ErrorBoundary";
import ThreeScene from "../visuals/ThreeScene";
import PlotlyChart from "../visuals/PlotlyChart";
import type { Data, Layout } from "plotly.js-dist-min";

interface PhysicsLabProps {
  className?: string;
}

interface Particle {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  vx: number;
  vy: number;
  mass: number;
  charge: number;
  color: string;
}

export default function PhysicsLab({ className }: PhysicsLabProps) {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<"simulation" | "waves" | "fields">("simulation");
  const [gravity, setGravity] = useState(9.8);
  const [timeScale, setTimeScale] = useState(1);
  const [showTrails, setShowTrails] = useState(true);
  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement>(null);
  const animFrameRef = useRef<number>(0);
  const particlesRef = useRef<Particle[]>([]);
  const trailsRef = useRef<Array<Array<{ x: number; y: number }>>>([]);

  const initParticles = useCallback((width: number, height: number) => {
    const particles: Particle[] = [];
    const trails: Array<Array<{ x: number; y: number }>> = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < 15; i++) {
      particles.push({
        x: Math.random() * width,
        y: Math.random() * height * 0.5,
        vx: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 4,
        vy: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2,
        mass: Math.random() * 2 + 1,
        charge: Math.random() > 0.5 ? 1 : -1,
        color: Math.random() > 0.5 ? "#60a5fa" : "#f87171",
      });
      trails.push([]);
    }

    particlesRef.current = particles;
    trailsRef.current = trails;
  }, []);

  const updateSimulation = useCallback((ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, width: number, height: number, dt: number) => {
    const particles = particlesRef.current;
    const trails = trailsRef.current;

    if (particles.length === 0) {
      initParticles(width, height);
      return;
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < particles.length; i++) {
      const p = particles[i];

      p.vy += gravity * dt * timeScale;

      p.x += p.vx * dt * 60;
      p.y += p.vy * dt * 60;

      if (p.x < 0 || p.x > width) {
        p.vx *= -0.9;
        p.x = Math.max(0, Math.min(width, p.x));
      }
      if (p.y > height) {
        p.vy *= -0.8;
        p.y = height;
        if (Math.abs(p.vy) < 1) p.vy = 0;
      }
      if (p.y < 0) {
        p.vy *= -0.9;
        p.y = 0;
      }

      if (showTrails) {
        trails[i].push({ x: p.x, y: p.y });
        if (trails[i].length > 50) trails[i].shift();
      } else {
        trails[i] = [];
      }
    }
  }, [gravity, timeScale, showTrails, initParticles]);

  const drawSimulation = useCallback((ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, width: number, height: number, dt: number) => {
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);

    const particles = particlesRef.current;
    const trails = trailsRef.current;

    for (let i = 0; i < particles.length; i++) {
      const p = particles[i];
      const trail = trails[i];

      if (showTrails && trail.length > 1) {
        ctx.strokeStyle = p.color + "40";
        ctx.lineWidth = 2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(trail[0].x, trail[0].y);
        for (let j = 1; j < trail.length; j++) {
          ctx.lineTo(trail[j].x, trail[j].y);
        }
        ctx.stroke();
      }

      ctx.fillStyle = p.color;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.mass * 4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();

      ctx.fillStyle = "#ffffff";
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(p.x - p.mass * 1.5, p.y - p.mass * 1.5, p.mass * 1.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    }
  }, [showTrails]);

  const drawWaves = useCallback((ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, width: number, height: number, time: number) => {
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);

    const cx = width / 2;
    const cy = height / 2;

    ctx.strokeStyle = "#334155";
    ctx.lineWidth = 1;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(0, cy);
    ctx.lineTo(width, cy);
    ctx.stroke();

    const waves = [
      { color: "#60a5fa", freq: 2, amp: 40, phase: 0 },
      { color: "#f87171", freq: 3, amp: 30, phase: Math.PI / 2 },
      { color: "#34d399", freq: 1.5, amp: 25, phase: Math.PI },
    ];

    waves.forEach((wave) => {
      ctx.strokeStyle = wave.color;
      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
      ctx.beginPath();

      for (let x = 0; x < width; x++) {
        const y = cy + Math.sin((x * wave.freq * Math.PI) / 180 + wave.phase + time * 2) * wave.amp * Math.exp(-Math.abs(x - cx) / (width * 0.3));
        if (x === 0) ctx.moveTo(x, y);
        else ctx.lineTo(x, y);
      }
      ctx.stroke();
    });

    ctx.fillStyle = "#94a3b8";
    ctx.font = "12px monospace";
    ctx.textAlign = "left";
    ctx.fillText("Wave Interference Pattern", 10, 20);
  }, []);

  const drawFields = useCallback((ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, width: number, height: number, time: number) => {
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);

    const cx = width / 2;
    const cy = height / 2;

    const charges = [
      { x: cx - 100, y: cy, charge: 1, color: "#f87171" },
      { x: cx + 100, y: cy, charge: -1, color: "#60a5fa" },
    ];

    charges.forEach((c) => {
      ctx.fillStyle = c.color;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(c.x, c.y, 12, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();

      ctx.fillStyle = "#ffffff";
      ctx.font = "bold 12px monospace";
      ctx.textAlign = "center";
      ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
      ctx.fillText(c.charge > 0 ? "+" : "-", c.x, c.y);
    });

    const gridSize = 30;
    for (let x = gridSize; x < width; x += gridSize) {
      for (let y = gridSize; y < height; y += gridSize) {
        let fx = 0, fy = 0;

        charges.forEach((c) => {
          const dx = x - c.x;
          const dy = y - c.y;
          const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
          if (dist < 20) return;
          const force = c.charge / (dist * dist);
          fx += (dx / dist) * force;
          fy += (dy / dist) * force;
        });

        const mag = Math.sqrt(fx * fx + fy * fy);
        if (mag > 0.001) {
          const scale = Math.min(15, 20 / mag);
          ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(148, 163, 184, ${Math.min(0.8, mag * 2)})`;
          ctx.lineWidth = 1;
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.moveTo(x, y);
          ctx.lineTo(x + (fx / mag) * scale, y + (fy / mag) * scale);
          ctx.stroke();
        }
      }
    }

    ctx.fillStyle = "#94a3b8";
    ctx.font = "12px monospace";
    ctx.textAlign = "left";
    ctx.fillText("Electric Field Lines", 10, 20);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    if (!canvas) return;

    const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
    if (!ctx) return;

    let cancelled = false;
    let lastTime = performance.now();

    const resize = () => {
      const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
      canvas.width = rect.width;
      canvas.height = rect.height;
      if (particlesRef.current.length === 0) {
        initParticles(canvas.width, canvas.height);
      }
    };
    resize();
    window.addEventListener("resize", resize);

    const animate = (now: number) => {
      if (cancelled) return;
      const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.05) * timeScale;
      lastTime = now;

      switch (activeTab) {
        case "simulation":
          updateSimulation(ctx, canvas.width, canvas.height, dt);
          drawSimulation(ctx, canvas.width, canvas.height, dt);
          break;
        case "waves":
          drawWaves(ctx, canvas.width, canvas.height, now * 0.001);
          break;
        case "fields":
          drawFields(ctx, canvas.width, canvas.height, now * 0.001);
          break;
      }

      animFrameRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
    };
    animFrameRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(animate);

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      cancelAnimationFrame(animFrameRef.current);
      window.removeEventListener("resize", resize);
    };
  }, [activeTab, timeScale, updateSimulation, drawSimulation, drawWaves, drawFields, initParticles]);

  const getPlotData = useCallback((): Data[] => {
    if (activeTab !== "simulation") return [];

    const particles = particlesRef.current;
    if (particles.length === 0) return [];

    return [
      {
        x: particles.map((p) => p.x),
        y: particles.map((p) => p.y),
        mode: "markers" as const,
        type: "scatter" as const,
        marker: {
          size: particles.map((p) => p.mass * 8),
          color: particles.map((p) => p.color),
          opacity: 0.7,
        },
      },
    ];
  }, [activeTab]);

  const plotLayout: Partial<Layout> = {
    paper_bgcolor: "transparent",
    plot_bgcolor: "transparent",
    font: { color: "#94a3b8" },
    margin: { t: 20, b: 40, l: 50, r: 20 },
    xaxis: { title: "X" },
    yaxis: { title: "Y", autorange: "reversed" },
  };

  return (
    <ErrorBoundary
      fallback={
        <div role="alert" aria-live="assertive" className="under-development">
          <span className="ud-icon" aria-hidden="true">⚠️</span>
          Physics visualization unavailable
        </div>
      }
    >
      <div className={className ?? "lab-3d-container"}>
        <div className="lab-tab-nav">
          <button
            className={`lab-tab-btn ${activeTab === "simulation" ? "active" : ""}`}
            onClick={() => setActiveTab("simulation")}
            aria-selected={activeTab === "simulation"}
            role="tab"
          >
            Particle Sim
          </button>
          <button
            className={`lab-tab-btn ${activeTab === "waves" ? "active" : ""}`}
            onClick={() => setActiveTab("waves")}
            aria-selected={activeTab === "waves"}
            role="tab"
          >
            Waves
          </button>
          <button
            className={`lab-tab-btn ${activeTab === "fields" ? "active" : ""}`}
            onClick={() => setActiveTab("fields")}
            aria-selected={activeTab === "fields"}
            role="tab"
          >
            Fields
          </button>
        </div>

        <canvas
          ref={canvasRef}
          className="three-scene"
          style={{ width: "100%", height: "100%" }}
          aria-label="Interactive physics simulation"
          role="img"
        />

        <div className="lab-results-grid" style={{ position: "absolute", bottom: 0, left: 0, right: 0, padding: "0.75rem", background: "rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.85)", backdropFilter: "blur(8px)" }}>
          <div className="lab-result-badge">
            <span className="label">Gravity</span>
            <span className="value">{gravity.toFixed(1)}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="lab-result-badge">
            <span className="label">Time Scale</span>
            <span className="value">{timeScale.toFixed(1)}x</span>
          </div>
          <div className="lab-result-badge">
            <span className="label">Trails</span>
            <span className="value">{showTrails ? "On" : "Off"}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="lab-result-badge">
            <span className="label">Particles</span>
            <span className="value">{particlesRef.current.length}</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div style={{ position: "absolute", top: 0.75rem, right: 0.75rem, display: "flex", gap: "0.5rem", flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
          <label style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "0.25rem", fontSize: "0.75rem", color: "#94a3b8" }}>
            <input
              type="range"
              min="0"
              max="20"
              step="0.1"
              value={gravity}
              onChange={(e) => setGravity(parseFloat(e.target.value))}
              aria-label="Gravity"
            />
            g:{gravity.toFixed(1)}
          </label>
          <label style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "0.25rem", fontSize: "0.75rem", color: "#94a3b8" }}>
            <input
              type="range"
              min="0.1"
              max="3"
              step="0.1"
              value={timeScale}
              onChange={(e) => setTimeScale(parseFloat(e.target.value))}
              aria-label="Time scale"
            />
            {timeScale.toFixed(1)}x
          </label>
          <label style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "0.25rem", fontSize: "0.75rem", color: "#94a3b8", cursor: "pointer" }}>
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={showTrails}
              onChange={(e) => setShowTrails(e.target.checked)}
              aria-label="Show trails"
            />
            Trails
          </label>
        </div>
      </div>
    </ErrorBoundary>
  );
}

] as const;

const MOLECULES: Record<string, { name: string; displayName: string; atoms: Array<{ symbol: string; pos: [number, number, number]; color: number; radius: number }>; bonds: Array<{ from: [number, number, number]; to: [number, number, number]; type: string }> }> = {
  water: { name: "H2O", displayName: "Water",
    atoms: [{ symbol:"O", pos:[0,0,0], color:0xef4444, radius:0.4 }, { symbol:"H", pos:[0.76,0,0], color:0xffffff, radius:0.28 }, { symbol:"H", pos:[-0.76,0,0], color:0xffffff, radius:0.28 }],
    bonds: [{ from:[0,0,0], to:[0.76,0,0], type:"single" }, { from:[0,0,0], to:[-0.76,0,0], type:"single" }] },
  co2: { name: "CO2", displayName: "Carbon Dioxide",
    atoms: [{ symbol:"C", pos:[0,0,0], color:0x52525b, radius:0.38 }, { symbol:"O", pos:[1.16,0,0], color:0xef4444, radius:0.35 }, { symbol:"O", pos:[-1.16,0,0], color:0xef4444, radius:0.35 }],
    bonds: [{ from:[0,0,0], to:[1.16,0,0], type:"double" }, { from:[0,0,0], to:[-1.16,0,0], type:"double" }] },
  methane: { name: "CH4", displayName: "Methane",
    atoms: [{ symbol:"C", pos:[0,0,0], color:0x52525b, radius:0.38 }, { symbol:"H", pos:[0.63,0.63,0.63], color:0xffffff, radius:0.25 }, { symbol:"H", pos:[-0.63,-0.63,0.63], color:0xffffff, radius:0.25 }, { symbol:"H", pos:[0.63,-0.63,-0.63], color:0xffffff, radius:0.25 }, { symbol:"H", pos:[-0.63,0.63,-0.63], color:0xffffff, radius:0.25 }],
    bonds: [{ from:[0,0,0], to:[0.63,0.63,0.63], type:"single" }, { from:[0,0,0], to:[-0.63,-0.63,0.63], type:"single" }, { from:[0,0,0], to:[0.63,-0.63,-0.63], type:"single" }, { from:[0,0,0], to:[-0.63,0.63,-0.63], type:"single" }] },
};

function PeriodicTable3D({ onSelectElement, searchQuery, filterCategory, autoRotate }: { onSelectElement: (el: typeof ALL_ELEMENTS[0]) => void; searchQuery: string; filterCategory: string; autoRotate: boolean }) {
  const mountRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    const mount = mountRef.current;
    if (!mount) return;
    let cancelled = false;
    (async () => {
      const THREE = await import("three");
      const { OrbitControls } = await import("three/examples/jsm/controls/OrbitControls.js");
      if (cancelled || !mount || mount.clientWidth === 0) return;
      const scene = new THREE.Scene();
      scene.background = new THREE.Color(0x0f172a);
      const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(50, mount.clientWidth / mount.clientHeight, 0.1, 1000);
      camera.position.set(12, 10, 16);
      camera.lookAt(7, 2, 0);
      const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true });
      renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));
      renderer.setSize(mount.clientWidth, mount.clientHeight);
      mount.appendChild(renderer.domElement);
      const controls = new OrbitControls(camera, renderer.domElement);
      controls.enableDamping = true;
      controls.autoRotate = autoRotate;
      controls.autoRotateSpeed = 0.5;
      scene.add(new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff, 0.6));
      scene.add(new THREE.GridHelper(30, 30, 0x334155, 0x1e293b).translateY(-1.5));
      const raycaster = new THREE.Raycaster();
      const mouse = new THREE.Vector2();
      let hoveredIdx = -1;

      function buildMeshes() {
        for (const obj of scene.children) { if ((obj as any)?.userData?.isElement) scene.remove(obj); }
        const boxGeo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.85, 1.1, 0.15);
        for (const el of ALL_ELEMENTS) {
          const color = CATEGORY_COLORS[el.category] ?? 0x6b7280;
          const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(boxGeo, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color, metalness: 0.3, roughness: 0.5, emissive: color, emissiveIntensity: 0.05 }));
          const isLanth = el.row === 9, isAct = el.row === 10;
          mesh.position.set((el.col - 1) * 1.05, (isLanth || isAct ? -(isAct ? 2 : 1) : el.row - 1) * 1.25, 0);
          mesh.userData = { element: el, index: el.number, isElement: true };
          scene.add(mesh);
        }
      }
      buildMeshes();

      const resize = () => {
        const w = mount.clientWidth, h = mount.clientHeight;
        if (w === 0 || h === 0) return;
        camera.aspect = w / h;
        camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
        renderer.setSize(w, h);
      };
      const observer = new ResizeObserver(resize);
      observer.observe(mount);

      function onMouseMove(e: MouseEvent) {
        if (!mount) return;
        const rect = mount.getBoundingClientRect();
        mouse.x = ((e.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width) * 2 - 1;
        mouse.y = -((e.clientY - rect.top) / rect.height) * 2 + 1;
        raycaster.setFromCamera(mouse, camera);
        const meshes = scene.children.filter((c) => (c as any)?.userData?.isElement) as THREE.Mesh[];
        const intersects = raycaster.intersectObjects(meshes);
        if (hoveredIdx !== -1) {
          const prev = meshes.find((m) => m.userData.index === hoveredIdx);
          if (prev?.material instanceof THREE.MeshStandardMaterial) { prev.material.emissiveIntensity = 0.05; prev.scale.setScalar(1); }
        }
        if (intersects.length > 0) {
          const mesh = intersects[0].object as THREE.Mesh;
          if (mesh.material instanceof THREE.MeshStandardMaterial) { mesh.material.emissiveIntensity = 0.5; mesh.scale.setScalar(1.2); }
          hoveredIdx = mesh.userData.index;
        } else { hoveredIdx = -1; }
      }
      function onClick() {
        if (hoveredIdx === -1) return;
        const mesh = (scene.children.filter((c) => (c as any)?.userData?.isElement) as THREE.Mesh[]).find((m) => m.userData.index === hoveredIdx);
        if (mesh?.userData?.element) onSelectElement(mesh.userData.element as typeof ALL_ELEMENTS[0]);
      }
      mount.addEventListener("mousemove", onMouseMove);
      mount.addEventListener("click", onClick);

      const animate = () => { if (!cancelled) { requestAnimationFrame(animate); controls.update(); renderer.render(scene, camera); } };
      animate();

      return () => {
        cancelled = true;
        observer.disconnect();
        controls.dispose();
        renderer.dispose();
        mount.removeEventListener("mousemove", onMouseMove);
        mount.removeEventListener("click", onClick);
        if (renderer.domElement.parentNode === mount) mount.removeChild(renderer.domElement);
        scene.traverse((obj: any) => { if (obj.geometry) obj.geometry.dispose(); if (obj.material) { if (Array.isArray(obj.material)) for (const m of obj.material) m.dispose(); else obj.material.dispose(); } });
      };
    })();
    return () => { cancelled = true; };
  }, [autoRotate]);

  return <div ref={mountRef} className="w-full rounded-xl border border-border bg-card overflow-hidden" style={{ height: "clamp(300px, 50vh, 600px)", width: "100%" }} />;
}

function MolecularModel3D({ moleculeKey }: { moleculeKey: string }) {
  const mountRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    const mount = mountRef.current;
    if (!mount) return;
    let cancelled = false;
    (async () => {
      const THREE = await import("three");
      const { OrbitControls } = await import("three/examples/jsm/controls/OrbitControls.js");
      const mol = MOLECULES[moleculeKey];
      if (!mol) return;
      const scene = new THREE.Scene();
      scene.background = new THREE.Color(0x0f172a);
      const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(50, mount.clientWidth / mount.clientHeight, 0.1, 1000);
      camera.position.set(4, 3, 5);
      camera.lookAt(0, 0, 0);
      const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true });
      renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));
      renderer.setSize(mount.clientWidth, mount.clientHeight);
      mount.appendChild(renderer.domElement);
      const controls = new OrbitControls(camera, renderer.domElement);
      controls.enableDamping = true;
      controls.autoRotate = true;
      controls.autoRotateSpeed = 1.5;
      scene.add(new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff, 0.5));
      const dl = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 1.4);
      dl.position.set(5, 8, 5);
      scene.add(dl);
      for (const atom of mol.atoms) {
        const m = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(atom.radius, 32, 24), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: atom.color, metalness: 0.2, roughness: 0.4, emissive: atom.color, emissiveIntensity: 0.1 }));
        m.position.set(...atom.pos);
        scene.add(m);
      }
      for (const bond of mol.bonds) {
        const from = new THREE.Vector3(...bond.from);
        const to = new THREE.Vector3(...bond.to);
        const dir = to.clone().sub(from);
        const len = dir.length();
        const center = from.clone().add(to).multiplyScalar(0.5);
        const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.06, 0.06, len, 12), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x94a3b8, metalness: 0.3, roughness: 0.6 }));
        mesh.position.copy(center);
        mesh.quaternion.setFromUnitVectors(new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0), dir.normalize());
        scene.add(mesh);
      }
      scene.add(new THREE.GridHelper(10, 20, 0x334155, 0x1e293b).translateY(-2));
      const resize = () => {
        const w = mount.clientWidth, h = mount.clientHeight;
        if (w === 0 || h === 0) return;
        camera.aspect = w / h;
        camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
        renderer.setSize(w, h);
      };
      const observer = new ResizeObserver(resize);
      observer.observe(mount);
      const animate = () => { if (!cancelled) { requestAnimationFrame(animate); controls.update(); renderer.render(scene, camera); } };
      animate();
      return () => {
        cancelled = true;
        observer.disconnect();
        controls.dispose();
        renderer.dispose();
        if (renderer.domElement.parentNode === mount) mount.removeChild(renderer.domElement);
      };
    })();
    return () => { cancelled = true; };
  }, [moleculeKey]);

  return <div ref={mountRef} className="w-full rounded-xl border border-border bg-card overflow-hidden" style={{ height: "clamp(300px, 50vh, 600px)", width: "100%" }} />;
}

function PeriodicTable2D({ onSelectElement, searchQuery, filterCategory }: { onSelectElement: (el: typeof ALL_ELEMENTS[number]) => void; searchQuery: string; filterCategory: string }) {
  const query = searchQuery.toLowerCase();
  const cat = filterCategory;
  const filtered = ALL_ELEMENTS.filter((el) => {
    const ms = !query || el.symbol.toLowerCase().includes(query) || el.name.toLowerCase().includes(query) || String(el.number).includes(query);
    const mc = !cat || el.category === cat;
    return ms && mc;
  });
  const rows = [{ num: 1 }, { num: 2 }, { num: 3 }, { num: 4 }, { num: 9, label: "Lanthanides" }, { num: 10, label: "Actinides" }];
  return (
    <div className="w-full overflow-x-auto">
      <div className="inline-flex flex-col gap-1 min-w-max">
        {rows.map((row) => {
          const els = filtered.filter((el) => el.row === row.num);
          if (els.length === 0) return null;
          return (
            <div key={row.num} className="flex items-center gap-1">
              <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 w-20 text-right pr-2">{row.label ?? `Period ${row.num}`}</span>
              <div className="flex gap-1">
                {Array.from({ length: 18 }, (_, i) => i + 1).map((col) => {
                  const el = els.find((e) => e.col === col);
                  if (!el) return <div key={col} className="w-[52px] h-[58px]" />;
                  const color = CATEGORY_COLORS[el.category] ?? 0x6b7280;
                  return (
                    <button key={col} className="w-[52px] h-[58px] rounded-lg border hover:border-slate-500 transition-all duration-200 flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-0.5 hover:scale-105 cursor-pointer"
                      style={{ backgroundColor: `#${color.toString(16).padStart(6, "0")}20`, borderColor: `#${color.toString(16).padStart(6, "0")}60` }}
                      onClick={() => onSelectElement(el)}>
                      <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">{el.number}</span>
                      <span className="text-sm font-bold" style={{ color: `#${color.toString(16).padStart(6, "0")}` }}>{el.symbol}</span>
                      <span className="text-[9px] text-slate-300 truncate w-full px-1 text-center">{el.name}</span>
                      <span className="text-[8px] text-slate-500">{el.mass}</span>
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function ChemistryLab() {
  const [selectedElement, setSelectedElement] = useState<typeof ALL_ELEMENTS[number] | null>(null);
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState("");
  const [filterCategory, setFilterCategory] = useState("");
  const [autoRotate, setAutoRotate] = useState(true);
  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<"3d" | "2d">("3d");
  const [selectedMolecule, setSelectedMolecule] = useState("water");

  const handleSelectElement = useCallback((el: typeof ALL_ELEMENTS[number]) => {
    setSelectedElement(el);
  }, []);

  const categories = [
    { key: "", label: "All" }, { key: "nonmetal", label: "Nonmetals" }, { key: "nobleGas", label: "Noble Gases" },
    { key: "alkaliMetal", label: "Alkali Metals" }, { key: "alkalineEarth", label: "Alkaline Earth" },
    { key: "metalloid", label: "Metalloids" }, { key: "halogen", label: "Halogens" },
    { key: "metal", label: "Metals" }, { key: "transitionMetal", label: "Transition Metals" },
    { key: "lanthanide", label: "Lanthanides" }, { key: "actinide", label: "Actinides" },
  ];

  return (
    <div className="w-full space-y-6">
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
        <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold">Chemistry Lab</h2>
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Interactive periodic table and molecular geometry visualizations.</p>
      </div>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3 items-center">
        <input type="text" placeholder="Search by name, symbol, or number..." value={searchQuery} onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
          className="w-full sm:w-64 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border border-border bg-card text-foreground placeholder-muted-foreground focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500/50" />
        <select value={filterCategory} onChange={(e) => setFilterCategory(e.target.value)}
          className="w-full sm:w-40 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border border-border bg-card text-foreground focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500/50">
          {categories.map((c) => <option key={c.key} value={c.key}>{c.label}</option>)}
        </select>
        <button onClick={() => setAutoRotate(!autoRotate)}
          className={`flex-1 min-w-[100px] px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border transition-all text-sm ${autoRotate ? "bg-blue-500/20 border-blue-500/50 text-blue-400" : "border-border text-muted-foreground hover:border-blue-500/30"}`}>
          Auto-Rotate {autoRotate ? "ON" : "OFF"}
        </button>
        <div className="flex rounded-lg border border-border overflow-hidden">
          {(["3d", "2d"] as const).map((m) => (
            <button key={m} onClick={() => setViewMode(m)}
              className={`flex-1 min-w-[80px] px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium transition-all ${viewMode === m ? "bg-muted text-foreground" : "bg-card text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"}`}>
              {m.toUpperCase()}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      <LabCard title="Periodic Table" subtitle="118 Elements">
        {viewMode === "3d" ? (
          <PeriodicTable3D onSelectElement={handleSelectElement} searchQuery={searchQuery} filterCategory={filterCategory} autoRotate={autoRotate} />
        ) : (
          <PeriodicTable2D onSelectElement={handleSelectElement} searchQuery={searchQuery} filterCategory={filterCategory} />
        )}
        <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {categories.filter((c) => c.key).map((cat) => (
            <div key={cat.key} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-muted-foreground cursor-pointer hover:text-foreground transition-colors"
              onClick={() => setFilterCategory(filterCategory === cat.key ? "" : cat.key)}>
              <div className="w-3 h-3 rounded-sm" style={{ backgroundColor: `#${CATEGORY_COLORS[cat.key]?.toString(16).padStart(6, "0") ?? "6b7280"}` }} />
              {cat.label}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </LabCard>

      {selectedElement && (
        <LabCard title={`${selectedElement.symbol} — ${selectedElement.name}`} subtitle={`#${selectedElement.number}`}>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 md:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
            <ResultBadge label="Atomic Mass" value={String(selectedElement.mass)} />
            <ResultBadge label="Category" value={CATEGORY_LABELS[selectedElement.category] ?? selectedElement.category} />
            <ResultBadge label="Phase" value={selectedElement.phase} />
            <ResultBadge label="Valency" value={String(selectedElement.valency)} />
          </div>
          <p className="mt-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground"><span className="font-semibold text-foreground">Electron Config:</span> <code className="text-blue-400">{selectedElement.electronConfig}</code></p>
          <button type="button" onClick={() => setSelectedElement(null)} className="mt-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground transition-colors">Close</button>
        </LabCard>
      )}

      <LabCard title="3D Molecular Models" subtitle="Sphere-and-stick representation">
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-4">
          <div className="flex-1"><MolecularModel3D moleculeKey={selectedMolecule} /></div>
          <div className="w-full sm:w-48 flex flex-col gap-3">
            <select value={selectedMolecule} onChange={(e) => setSelectedMolecule(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full rounded-md border border-border bg-card px-3 py-1.5 text-sm text-foreground focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500/50">
              {Object.entries(MOLECULES).map(([key, mol]) => <option key={key} value={key}>{mol.displayName} ({mol.name})</option>)}
            </select>
            <MeaningPanel title={MOLECULES[selectedMolecule]?.displayName ?? "Molecule"}
              meaning="Molecular geometry determines chemical reactivity. VSEPR theory predicts shape from electron pair repulsion."
              points={["H2O: bent (104.5 deg), polar", "CO2: linear (180 deg), nonpolar", "CH4: tetrahedral (109.5 deg), nonpolar"]} color="green" />
            <CollapsibleControls label="Atom Colors">
              <div className="space-y-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                <p><span className="text-red-400">●</span> Oxygen (O)</p>
                <p><span className="text-gray-400">●</span> Carbon (C)</p>
                <p><span className="text-white">●</span> Hydrogen (H)</p>
              </div>
            </CollapsibleControls>
          </div>
        </div>
      </LabCard>
    </div>
  );
}
